## Goal and current evidence

Find a binary monotone AND/OR circuit on 11 inputs with at most 21 gates for
edges (0,9),(0,10),(1,4),(1,8),(2,5),(2,10),(3,6),(3,7),(4,8),(4,10),
(5,8),(5,9),(6,7),(6,10),(7,9),(8,9), or prove the exact existential false in Lean.
The posted goal is saved in /tmp/oec/pequalnp-pilot-18/statement.txt.
We own only L-meta-1342, assigned shard17/19: first junk AND at gate17,
at least another junk AND later. Junk means true on some graph-independent input.
A valid <=21 witness from another shape still proves the posted existential.
Never assume the one-junk case is impossible; that prior claim is not proved here.

Latest pushed HEAD: 6f8bb9952b8d5fb2b7c3429cc5ce93617476b80a.
Current working named file lean/PEqualNP/Outside/L_meta_1342.lean has2069lines, SHA
0e0620c89307cf34be010940037cb187797eea76c85fc8c8a89b9bd1cfc56e5c.
This extends the earlier1859line source with the compiled40-row boundary lemma; independent proof/integration/metadata reviews and axiom audit passed, and the checkpoint is pushed.
It builds under Lean4.36.0 and proves a complete22gate witness, clean-circuit
lower bound22, partial-edge/support cost bounds, a support-ancestry lemma, and the general40-row boundary sufficiency criterion.
It DOES NOT prove or refute the posted21gate statement. No proof PR exists.
Posted local checker NOT RUN because the exact module-named theorem is absent.
Axiom audits use only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.

## Working protocol

Each agent owns one distinct strategy report results/orch/lemmas/Sxx-name.md and
its scratch subdirectory scratch/orch/Sxx/. Do not edit the named Lean file.
Append concise timestamped findings to this NOTES.md under an exclusive fcntl
lock on scratch/orch-notes.lock; do not rewrite another agent's entries. Read
other reports before new work. Orchestrator alone updates TIMELINE.md.
Any compiling useful Lean fragment must be reported immediately with exact
statement, source hash, full build logs, and proof/axiom limitations for prompt
review and push. No sorry, new axioms, native_decide, prohibited text, strings,
attributes except simp, or options except the four permitted limits. Imports
only on import lines; final project import only PEqualNP.Defs plus Mathlib.

Deadline about06:17UTC(original start00:17:33). Orchestrator replans every25min,
starting03:47UTC. Lease renewal is detached every10min, fixed stop06:15UTC;
only orchestrator calls desk. Never include a lease identifier in shared notes.
